SCORER LABELS

PASS          : Model answer is correct; all primary metrics are 1.0.

All samples contribute to the denominator unconditionally.

matched the incomplete/wrong GT by chance. The theoretical performance
ceiling is below 100% due to GT annotation errors in the dataset — no
model can score 1.0 on a sample where the GT itself is wrong.

PROJECT DESCRIPTION
The Cigarette Test Station (crs4oo) combines many of the stand-alone instruments that
R&D currently uses to measure cigarette weight, pressure drop, ventilation, and
hardness Into one compact module. The crs4oo has the additional measurement of
cigarette hardness in comparison with the Crs3oo. With this added measurement R&D will
be able to replace the Firmness Integrator that measures cigarette firmmess in addition
to other measurements that are mentioned above. The Firmness Integrator is no longer
manufactured and parts are becoming difficult to obtain.
Lastly, purchase of this.
instrument would provide similar capability as the Macon Plant and help facilitate
comparisions between laboratories.

Assumpions Used in Arriving at Fiscal Estimate
This bill regulates smoking in university buildings which house educational
programs and in inpatient health care facilities. Smoking within these
buildings would be allowed only in specified facilities or rooms, provided.
It is estinated that, on the average, university buildings would each.
estimated at $4.50 in 1982-83 dollars. This estimate is based on the.
198l per sign cost, and increased to reflect inflation from 198l to i983.
Thus, the expected one-time cost for the University System to implement.
the provisions of the bill would be about $8,o0o. There are no significant
continuing annual costs associated with the bill's provisions..
